# Incumbent switch checklists

These separate inventories record verified workflows in selected official sources. **Inventory coverage is incomplete for all three incumbents.** Other documented workflows may be omitted. Sampled pages, a sitemap, partner referrals and Terrace product boundaries cannot establish completeness.

Every inventoried workflow below remains a **Gap** until current passed replacement acceptance proves its actor, public door, prerequisites and outcome. A related DONE clause, generic CSV importer, staff-recorded substitute or boundary statement alone does not establish replacement. This page grants no migration, payment, provider or operational authority.

The [source-corpus manifest](switch-checklist-sources/manifest.json) retains separate inventories, captured-source hashes, source-to-workflow reconciliation and unaudited source areas. Source quotations are documentary claims by the incumbent; they establish no Terrace capability or provider entitlement.

## Corpus coverage and uncertainty

Public HTTPS response captured on the stated UTC date. Capture text decodes HTML entities, drops script, style, noscript and SVG contents, collapses whitespace by block, omits addresses and expands ambiguous word slash pairs. Raw response and retained normalized text have separate SHA-256 hashes. Hashes identify captures, not present-day source currency. Browser-rendered sources explicitly retain hashes for the initial HTTP response, the loaded document and the normalized visible article text; a loading shell is never substituted for the article.

## AppFolio checklist

**Partial official-source inventory. 247 workflows; additional source areas remain unaudited.**

### AppFolio required named gaps

**Corporate accounting — Gap:** Company books and financial reporting remain unqualified replacements; residential accounting and product boundaries cannot remove this gap.

Documented in [Corporate accounting · af-accounting-01](#corporate-accounting-af-accounting-01) — [official source](https://www.appfolio.com/property-manager/accounting-reporting), captured 2026-11-05, section “Corporate Accounting”, line 43.

**Telephone maintenance intake — Gap:** Documented call intake remains a gap. Online repair forms, staff entry and provider referrals do not replace telephone maintenance intake.

Documented in [Telephone maintenance intake · af-smart-blog-01](#telephone-maintenance-intake-af-smart-blog-01) — [official source](https://www.appfolio.com/blog/smart-maintenance), captured 2026-11-05, section “So how does it work?”, line 21.

**Technician GPS — Unqualified source coverage:** Official-source coverage of this capability remains unaudited or inaccessible. No absence, replacement or complete inventory is inferred.

**Scheduled reports — Unqualified source coverage:** Official-source coverage of this capability remains unaudited or inaccessible. No absence, replacement or complete inventory is inferred.

> The Smart Maintenance Contact Center uses intelligent AI to automate responses to residents 24/7 — no matter the time of day or night, so your team can focus on running your business. Long gone are the days of you or your residents having to fill out stacks of paperwork if something breaks. With multiple forms of communication, residents can easily submit a text message, make a quick call, or use their online resident portal to get started. From there, the request is received and the resident gets an AI-aided response within minutes to follow up and determine the urgency of the issue.

#### Maintenance emergency troubleshooting and preferred-vendor dispatch · af-smart-blog-03

**Gap:** Same-capability replacement acceptance is unqualified for maintenance emergency troubleshooting and preferred-vendor dispatch. Related native evidence does not qualify the documented actor, door, prerequisites and outcome.

**Related replacements:** [AGENT-003](#clause-agent-003) (missing); [WORK-001](#clause-work-001) (partial); [WORK-004](#clause-work-004) (done)

> “Smart Maintenance monitors all work orders that come in, day or night, to see if it’s an emergency and helps residents troubleshoot or decide any next steps to ensure the property is not being damaged or that there are no health and safety issues. Then, the system contacts our preferred vendors, a choice that we put within the system. So ultimately, Smart Maintenance can take that work order and handle the situation without contacting anyone on our team. Right now with the staffing shortage that is going on nationwide, it’s just one extra support that you can have for those team members so they aren’t working overtime every single week.”

> Once selected, a new screen will show the typical columns that are contained in the rent roll — Unit, Tenant, Status, Rent, Deposit, Lease Start, Lease End, etc. Below this, the user will see a section for adding columns from other reports. You could do so by finding their phone number and email address in this section and adding it to the base report. This new report can then be exported or saved for future use.

**Technician GPS — Unqualified source coverage:** Official-source coverage of this capability remains unaudited or inaccessible. No absence, replacement or complete inventory is inferred.
